I just… most days it’s coffee, maybe a protein bar between jobs, then a small dinner. Full meals make me feel kind of slowed down, especially when I’m working.\n\nDoctor: How much weight have you lost, and has this happened recently or over a longer period of time?\n\nPatient: I’m not really sure on the number. The insurance doctor just said it was too low. I guess it’s been over time, not like all at once… but I’ve always been lean with the work, being on ladders all day and long hours.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any unintentional weight loss recently, or has your weight stayed about the same over time?\n\nPatient: I haven’t really noticed it like that. The insurance doctor said my weight was too low, but to me I’ve just always looked lean… being on ladders all day, working long days.\n\nDoctor: Would you say your weight has dropped below what is normal for you, or has it always been this way?\n\nPatient: I mean… I came in because the insurance physical doctor said it was too low. To me it’s kind of always seemed like I’m just lean from work, being on ladders all day and doing long days.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost more than 5% of your body weight over the past 6 months without trying?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know the percentage. I didn’t come in because I noticed that—just the insurance physical doctor said my weight was too low. To me I’ve always just looked lean from the ladders and long workdays.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me approximately how much you weigh now, and has your weight changed compared to a year ago?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know the exact number offhand. The insurance physical is where they said it was too low. Compared to last year… I guess I haven’t noticed much myself. I just figured I’m lean from working, climbing ladders all day.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me what your current BMI or weight was noted as during the insurance physical, and was it significantly below the normal range for your height?\n\nPatient: I don’t remember the BMI number, honestly. I didn’t really think of it that way—I just figured I’m lean from work, being on ladders all day and doing long days.\n\nDoctor: Was your BMI during the insurance physical below 18.5, which is considered the lower limit of the normal range?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure if it was below 18.5 exactly.
